                                💡 AI Executive Summary: Key News Insights

                            <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" width="100%" style="background-color: #F8FAFC; border-left: 4px solid #FF9900; border: 1px solid #E2E8F0; border-left-width: 4px;">

                                    <td style="padding: 20px 24px; font-size: 13.5px; color: #334155; line-height: 1.7em; text-align: justify;">
                                        <div style='margin-top:2px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>1. AWS 서울 리전 클로드 5 출시와 소버린 데이터 주권 및 종속성 딜레마</div>아마존웹서비스(AWS)가 서울 리전에 앤트로픽의 최신 인공지능 모델 '클로드 오퍼스 5'와 '소넷 5'를 전격 출시함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiaEFVX3lxTE81V3FDQ2pSdFFOOHYzTzh0UVRsYkNTLVVqdmJ6QXdCbGFwa2x4YjFKTzR3N01iTlN2T3lhRWxKdjZKWGJRQWgtdGRUVHVlT3htb1loN2dURlJqYXZGd3RNNGZGWWRxaGhI?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[1]</a>. 이번 조치로 데이터 국외 반출 규제에 묶였던 국내 금융 및 공공 부문은 해외 전송 없이 국내 리전에서 AI 추론을 자체 처리하며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMigAFBVV95cUxQYXpjWmZfSm5DZjlzNndPY0Y1UHU3MDR4bUJOcjFlaDF6WlRDZlZ6UkhFOTMwZWdwTWgyV3ZtdUhQT1B6OG5vUGMwUjBGdTJ6d2UyYmhyMkt3TXd3Z1JzM0lzMUE3RnRLcmpVZnJGcEg2ZVg3WU5xZlh4d19kTUdvUQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[2]</a> 데이터 주권과 보안 요건을 충족하는 소버린 인프라 환경을 확보함 <a href='https://www.aitimes.com/news/articleView.html?idxno=215807' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[3]</a>. 이에 발맞춰 삼성전자 디바이스솔루션(DS) 부문은 개발자 1만 명에게 '클로드 코드'를 도입하고 <a href='https://n.news.naver.com/mnews/article/138/0002243200' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[4]</a> AWS를 통해 보안 권한과 비용을 통합 통제함 <a href='https://n.news.naver.com/mnews/article/138/0002243200' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[4]</a>. 글로벌 시장에서도 AWS가 인도 리전으로 클로드 추론을 확대하고 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Amazon Bedrock expands Claude model availability to in-country inferencing in India | Artificial Intelligence&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[5]</a> 인도 블루 클라우드 소프트텍이 '블루엣지'를 출시하는 등 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiwgFBVV95cUxPaEVsVEV3VWlXd0w5NFRobDBTT0dwakFFUnlkb1Fpc3ZCUVJfMmdfUlRBMXJNLXhSeTBBWURjV2ZYVWE3bGpud3hmZDU3YkM1VFBWUk1MZ25tWjZzakVsZ3A3bzM0ZTRnR0lOWGxGMVdrSjBvVXd1TTViQmRKajRJbWF1em9SRkFsUkM3U0Y0RGEzX1YyRzhZdzhxUHFaanFSU2h1Vjl4S0ZfMkx2NDBJeXZsallNRGUzLW9fMlhoY0oxQQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[6]</a> 국경 내 통제권 확보가 가속화됨 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMizgFBVV95cUxOUWM4NThxcGhPYkJ3WV9qNEJvUlkxQncwbmJUWTZhb2w4V1JaSEw3WldtOENVd19hekNvRDQtWEdTOXotellzZDVDcUpLRlB6YjNPV0Nnd016QkxXV3cxeDl1M0xrZEZvMTdkclJZeHB6RmpvLUdRU3ppZ1BuZWYteGVyR2w3MzMwbnBMNHduV0NTZm51Mm1uYWY4aXNGUXd3V3l1Ump3aTJSbWhmQ1BDZWxMLTE3QXNGQjlvTFFvUDZrNVFMOTdkcXhqV0IzQdIB2wFBVV95cUxOeWpEUktQWFhoMTYwU1NmdWdXSi14dnJSLVZHSThVMVlzdWVvaHRZbGNaMVVOS2x4bV9od0hlQTk3MkpzU2FsdHZNLXJnT0FHektsdEFNeG1NNE5ZMVRLejBnM0RVZDlOb3ZRNU1XZk5leGJyTS00MldpY0NBaVM3cmxYZ1RFNlc4b0VjSXYxYUpCa2R5cFVwM2l3bE5zV3RtZXJTcEFkUlNkdnJsUjlHX3RDbE1lcXI2MFJxb09CT2lnUlJJZjFUT3BtNzJSSFpIOGI0RjBkLWVoT1k?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[7]</a>. 반면 앤트로픽의 기업공개(IPO) 과정에서 전체 매출의 47%가 아마존과 구글 클라우드에 집중되고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMitwJBVV95cUxOT3FNQ0tkNUs5YWhEVDllcDBVS3ZyOXFYdW1kT2FrSklKRUFoeDZ3V3Q4YktMaEhKdzBNLU5FUklqcUJXR01qNWwtY2tiWmxYanB3dkNBMDFUVk5wUm1sV3RBNl8xeWhkSDA0SFdnSjAzdmRDeUxPWDhHUVpWQ1VreFVhTGpfZ1dUZHpwbktlSHJtemhFVThUcnBsWTR3S0EzWWtkZE5fSW8yV3FYUm9VZmlfMExiRUx6cUdfTGo1UzN4aVhVVURCOGU3T092WGFnQlFQcGJseEJNZTYxelJJMFI0WUR5Ylp4ajI3SkdoWFhUYTltYVQ2ZE1WY0hQdjVobm5xc2N2TDM1azZ5UW5BcmxqN2ZFSlJkbk5UaDRzQWF0YUMzM1VueUFzbTc3UTN1OC1kdGJ5MA?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[8]</a> 구독 비중은 20% 미만에 그친 구조가 공개되며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMi0wFBVV95cUxNZm9Vb3BrbnZadWRlUUZCNGVDQTlwN20xdnowSnhHRjl1eWR6WGgtanl5QWowNjFMNDRiazRveFZuY0FhTGMxNy1paUlKR1o1eEw5UXlyakNabmF0dXhZRmp2NDVmM0ozRE5vTGY4YmIyQm9XTnFJR2dyRTYyd1QtQmthcjhoVHBneG1TdzJxV1M4NTdzQ0JYdWF1Z2NNY3ppSGZZbnJFb0hiY1daMVRvN3p3VmhNeHgwdjBjSzM4cHlzWTlVcFF5M2pYaHdEYmRFSVdz?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[9]</a> 빅테크 인프라 종속 리스크가 주요 한계로 지적됨 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiowJBVV95cUxNMDBwQnhMMXZnRHpuRmNyQk03bkM4aUtvckViX241REs1NWdLYmg5S3F1QUFwM29IWG5Wdjd0Vy1yeGwzZVVqckNiM1JwS1RtZk82UzRFWlB2UjgzREhJMXJvZXdIdGJxRDlsNzlmTDhncXRxb20wWmVXLTVpNkY0M3NCVVRFUE5NOTFPSG5lcWJQWGV2aTVXaEl6dFNTdVpnMVJVUmR1eHFaNnFaSFpjam1DR0R5bS1RM0t3eEp2bUsxVmlsNEZjRUNWQ1lhdW1qYjNMbk1oRTBVNVliNW8xWXJFdEh2cXVjWEhDbjROZjlFRXM3M2dEeDRPNnl3S0RVMWJHa3hnTlJfNm95bVlDc3VCU3dGMXg3cV9MM3RnT09yNW8?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[10]</a>.<div style='margin-top:18px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>2. 조립식 모듈러 AIDC와 4MW 풀스택 AI 팩토리 공세</div>AI 가속기 집적도 폭증에 대응해 공장 사전 제작형 모듈러 인프라가 차세대 데이터센터의 핵심 표준으로 급부상함. LG CNS는 싱가포르 'DCWA 2026'에서 엔비디아 베라 루빈 기반 4MW급 사전제작 모듈형 데이터센터(PMDC)를 전격 공개하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMickFVX3lxTE5rSnFEdjdUUEdmaGVFdVc4WjdhSXMtby1EQ2lXcWNweWJ3VUxSVlZDZXpfWlNCdF9zbDFVbG52cDNCSkR4aWl4WFk5SGF3aFRUQjNlQkJaSE1MTzVGZkVyanM4eHZYSnE5Ukd0R3RIbnRVUQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[11]</a> 부지 제약을 해소하는 'AI 팩토리 풀스택' 서비스를 제시함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMibEFVX3lxTFAxVVRleUpaWUpPdFVKSjBTdlF3QVdHbnhfVXZVSzduNFBYMW5YMWwxSE5vd05wS0h2TkNlYnVvSXo4RE51UXdaZFpLVWVDa0ctVkI3Nk80bGVycW9oRWNHaE94SXR0bWs2dFMxcA?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[12]</a>. 컨테이너 구조물에 전력과 공조 설비를 일체화해 구축 기간을 대폭 줄이며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiXEFVX3lxTFBReENYT09LWDdOV0R6aVRhYTRfcmFUcHlqSTdWRkRSTlFtNVhhMWFscFFDMUQ5V0NiNzE0UzFNN1RQN3dpWWtwT25lYWJqQ3FocVI0WkFzMXBWRDlO?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[13]</a> 그룹 역량을 결집한 글로벌 턴키 수주를 정조준함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiVkFVX3lxTE9PX0ZpODFLZS1sdi14cjZpUmpIdGI0eDJKdmJiWTQ5M0tLdFBPSWZMSThMOHlzeEEtcnFaMFdRTzdUMk9laDRWVm9TM25iTXY5Q184SVVB?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[14]</a>. 아울러 삼송 센터에 랙당 200kW 초고밀도 액체냉각을 구축해 실증 역량을 확보함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMic0FVX3lxTE9EeFN6MEdlS2RlT2gtWkNDSzJaTFI3dXdjOXpseThRTVBHV3lSYnR0Nl9NS0Y0aEI2OWNVU0JTYk0zMV9ZU0lCaFRYU2NaVGpQVHFaSm9yd0JOdXpBU0NqNUZ6MjJQWHlSUkoxXzRab1BYYkXSAXdBVV95cUxNcnRoNkpiTVhjSXdEU0E1SkRMbEVRbWJVdUJIVVpfSFFKRkNsRE1aLWtES2ozQXFrdzdZUnFwdXJ2clF4UVNCOEMzak5vUjdNeG85WHU2MV9SMHNlZFFmM3c5amFTbnBqVk5zSGgybFBaZG9xTDIzTQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[15]</a>. 국내 스타트업 엘리스그룹 역시 3개월 만에 짓고 비용을 40% 절감하는 모듈형 PMDC를 앞세워 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMidEFVX3lxTE9MU0NSVDM1SEdmdG1WUlRPU2YwbTN1RnY4Wm5kLTBuY2FZcFlsczF1Y3dPZWNfTG5DcktpVUJULVJIZHFsNmk2ZHU1RVhQYk1OZUFrZWZKX2FmQk1LVkJJMzMtSDE4amNoM0FoY2szTEtEbW5n?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[16]</a> 기업공개(IPO) 추진과 네오클라우드 공략을 선언함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiT0FVX3lxTFBUV00tYWdmN3ZteXV4Q25jdVRwUmxkbTVDTnJqVGw2TTZSamkzMU1WMnlNVk1vQkRqMVM5bmtmOWFCVFFVNkw4S2F5bUQwMVE?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[17]</a>. 글로벌 시장에서도 트레인이 차세대 AIDC용 800V 직류(DC) 냉각 시스템을 시연하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMicEFVX3lxTE53cFFIclJ6bkE5ZURZZnR5YjlzQWJXRDhfUExQV1c1WElJMzJEcDhDdnZ3ZjRpZVpvT3ctMjRDMVR3STFXaDFVdE1Pc3ZRQ1ZiZ3JKYzNLY05UcXF5dXNTSHZPM2JKQjRvb2xBdDY5b3Q?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[18]</a> 오텍캐리어가 글로벌 캐리어와 냉각 협력을 강화하는 등 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiakFVX3lxTE42VXJNMzJva3ByVHhxNVlBVjNzdE9QZHk5RHhMQUlOTjJIN2V5c3FPUXZaNk55d3Y0cFdTY0pBXzR0UDBsOENBVjYyM21qZ1dRdUtoMWRMWTRhcXlJTUNNUFdDTTZYRld2V3fSAWpBVV95cUxONlVyTTMyb2twclR4cTVZQVYzc3RPUGR5OUR4TEFJTk4ySDdleXNxT1F2WjZOeXd2NHBXU2NKQV80dFAwbDhDQVY2MjNtamdXUXVLaDFkTFk0YXF5SU1DTVBXQ002WEZXdld3?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[19]</a> 초고압 직류화와 모듈러 냉각이 인프라 필수 규격으로 안착함.<div style='margin-top:18px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>3. 베라 루빈 NVL72 클라우드 상용화와 10억 달러 자체 칩 동맹</div>초거대 AI 워크로드 확장에 발맞춰 글로벌 네오클라우드 선도 기업 코어위브(CoreWeave)가 엔비디아 '베라 루빈 NVL72' 클라우드 서비스를 전격 출시함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiswFBVV95cUxPcjhlQXBBclR0SjJkTFJEdGJURndXU3hBbm91M1FBNHB4eDZIcG5BNlFDWl9kTFVzOUpPNlF5dkE4djBsdkhfM0lhTTZxeVpXa0FtU0lHNWtiUWE1TW5idXllZnE3QjhhRG1IaVB1N2wxUHJGSnJUZ2pmMlI2WndjNXRWRUsxanhLOGdvNVFiMkVHdE1oZ0QtWnlCaWkzeERPeWpTaURPZXVXN3BJWjk4TkUtcw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[20]</a>. 코어위브는 첫 고객사인 코그니션(Cognition) 환경에서 기존 대비 4.8배의 연산 처리량 향상을 입증했으며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMirwFBVV95cUxQQ1Fqb1NSZ1FnRm9nU1ZGamx1VncxeTFzY0xxSTdJY0h3X1FvRTRna0ZJMXB5cUMyRVEtQURlOGdaYjRGbVQweFgwb0RnRzhRelFFSXpwMWNRLW1tR0ZkOWpaOGNLYWxqdElMaWF4emVBdFg1QTBLUFJ5WWVPcHhTRVhqVGlpWUY0R2lWbHpsQ0Q4MFZxQk9ZelNhQjB5RF9aVFk2ZGFlNVNUbHZ4b3dn?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[21]</a> 1만 1,264개 코어로 3배 빠른 엔비디아 '베라 CPU' 클라우드까지 포트폴리오에 추가함 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=CoreWeave’s NVIDIA Vera CPU: 11,264 Cores, 3x Faster (2026)&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[22]</a>. 한편 엔비디아 의존도를 낮추려는 아마존(AWS)은 반도체 IP 1위 시놉시스(Synopsys)와 10억 달러(약 1조 3,500억 원) 이상의 다년 맞춤형 실리콘 IP 및 EDA 라이선스 계약을 체결함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMicEFVX3lxTFBZeHlXc0laNldwemFNWHpWNEx0SUVHSVVVbGZ6LTJwYVdfeWdyQURXNE5Rd193ZTlHbWFRZ0JiMUMzcGV0NENHRXI0bnItbjlQNTJwTEs5b25MNk5RUE41YUk5WnFiWmFHTVVZSm1tczQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[23]</a>. 이를 통해 AWS는 트레이니움 등 자체 차세대 AI 칩 설계를 가속화하며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMimwFBVV95cUxQUzdSSV9VNzFsdVdIdE43WXJKRkpqbHdrcWp4RmZaVEZGN3hjWTBhYkY0WFo0U1drQVNUaTh1Q0Rhc1RpaWpnSkVHeUl6dm1rX1BZZFREaWZpenlBN2xYUGVHbElIYlJIY2x0cWNiakdSdUU3TnpWSDNXYXFmVU1IM29Wcy1nd1c4bHJvck5fY0lWUEtCS3BCcVl4aw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[26]</a> 네비우스(Nebius)가 AIB 데이터센터와 50MW 공급 계약을 확정하는 등 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMicEFVX3lxTE5hNEhwMzFzVGdmT1hxMHdKQThBR2VkRXo2ckV4ckhJbWxCVk12MDFRSFl5bG5fbC02RXczNE5UQVZibXZmWmFTVm5md2ZSajFRbTdYZVpRVFhYcDBsVDNpY3NaYTNwZXA4aWpRQkU4MWM?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[27]</a> 특화 클라우드 진영의 설비투자가 대폭 확대됨.<div style='margin-top:18px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>4. 미 상원의 전력비 법안 부결과 세제·비밀주의 둘러싼 정치·규제 후폭풍</div>데이터센터의 에너지 소비 급증이 주민 전기요금을 압박한다는 우려 속에 미국 연방 상원이 데이터센터 전력비의 소비자 전가를 막는 공화당의 '전기요금 납부자 보호법(허스티드 법안)'을 57대 43으로 전격 부결시킴 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMia0FVX3lxTE9haDNtaGkzSjJfcXhBSEpqYlBESkFiQkZkdEFxX2hQaUNuVzh0VzdxNDViclR5S2g5S1E4OWVBak0tR3VYT09rN2JMTU1xQy1uTkRzQVhUVXlublAzOU0xRXE2ZWhJaC1PRTlR?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[31]</a> 미 의회가 빅테크의 전력·용수 비밀유지계약(NDA) 조사에 착수했으며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMie0FVX3lxTE5UZlR1TmJQVEdwRVA3SFB4YW40TTFGVGRaYm5QOV9GZ1I1U3ZWQnBOa0V4WnBjeWFvVFFTbkZzNXQyV0w3WjNxNWZsaUlPMGRMT0U4VHVINUNXRkwzMjctS2RKaGxxR1h0dUEwUkhEUVpXNHEzRWFBNElCMA?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[35]</a>. 또한 강원도 동해시 120조 원 프로젝트의 전력 지중선로 설명회에서 전자파와 소음 반발이 터져 나오는 등 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiWkFVX3lxTFB0VWtuNWJaVlhZbEZwVkxRbnV0SGpNYWpoczFEemRkWFA3WF9YNTNXWXFFcVdaSGhGajlhckRQaF8tcUNyME1kYWNFNVhVMjlWa3BaN1JSWVBZQdIBWkFVX3lxTFB0VWtuNWJaVlhZbEZwVkxRbnV0SGpNYWpoczFEemRkWFA3WF9YNTNXWXFFcVdaSGhGajlhckRQaF8tcUNyME1kYWNFNVhVMjlWa3BaN1JSWVBZQQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[37]</a>.
